So that we have
(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},u_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},p_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(-2)})=0,
from which, we infer

(4.13)

\quad\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\partial_{\tau}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}=0,\quad x\in\Omega,\tau>0,\\
\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}=0,\\
\partial_{\tau}u_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\nabla p_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(-1)}=0,\\
\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits u_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}=0.\end{array}\right.

We implement the system (
4.13
) with the boundary conditions:

(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},u_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},p_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(-1)})(x^{\prime},y,\tau\rightarrow+\infty)=0.

Thus we have
(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},u_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},p_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(-1)})=0
. By plugging them into the next order, we obtain

(4.14)

\quad\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\partial_{\tau}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}=z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}),\quad x\in\Omega,\tau>0,\\
-\Delta\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}=\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},\\
\partial_{\tau}u_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\nabla p_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}=0,\\
\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits u_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}=0.\end{array}\right.

To match the initial boundary condition (
\mathbf{EN}
), we implement the system (
4.14
) with
the conditions:

(4.15)

\begin{split}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},y,\tau=0)=-\rho^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},y,t=0)=\Delta\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(t=0),\\
(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},u_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},p_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)})(x^{\prime},y,\tau\rightarrow+\infty)=0\quad\hbox{and}\quad\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|_{\partial\Omega}=0.\end{split}

It’s easy to see that
u_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}=p_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}=0
. Now observing that
z_{1}c_{1}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+z_{2}c_{2}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}=0
, one has

\partial_{\tau}(\frac{c_{\scriptscriptstyle 1,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}}{D_{1}}+\frac{c_{\scriptscriptstyle 2,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}}{D_{2}})=0.

Thus we get
\frac{c_{\scriptscriptstyle 1,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}}{D_{1}}+\frac{c_{\scriptscriptstyle 2,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}}{D_{2}}=0
,
\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}=\frac{z_{1}D_{1}-z_{2}D_{2}}{D_{1}}c_{\scriptscriptstyle 1,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}
and then rewrite the equation of
\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}
as

(4.16)

\partial_{\tau}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}=z_{1}(z_{1}D_{1}-z_{2}D_{2})\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits\bigl(c_{\scriptscriptstyle 1}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\bigr).

The next proposition is concerned with the exponential decay of
(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})
.

Proposition 4.1
.

Let
c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)\in H^{5}
and
c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(0)\in H^{2}.
Then the system (
4.14
-
4.15
) admits a unique solution in
C^{\infty}([0,+\infty);H^{2})
.
Moreover, there exist positive constants
M_{1}
,
M_{2}
depending only on
\lambda,z_{i}
and
D_{i}
for
i=1,2
, such that for
0\leq l\leq 3
, we have

\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|\partial_{\tau}^{l}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(\tau)\|^{2}_{H^{2}}+\|\partial_{\tau}^{l}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(\tau)\|^{2}_{H^{4}}\leq M_{1}(\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)\|_{H^{5}}^{12}+1)\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(0)\|^{2}_{H^{2}}e^{-M_{2}\tau}.

Proof.

For simplicity, we only deal with the
a priori
estimate for the system (
4.14
).
We get, by taking
L^{2}
inner product of (
4.16
) with
\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}
, that

\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{d\tau}\|\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+z_{1}(z_{1}D_{1}-z_{2}D_{2})\int_{\Omega}c_{\scriptscriptstyle 1}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)|\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|^{2}\,dx=0.

Due to
c_{\scriptscriptstyle 1}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)\geq\lambda>0
, we achieve

\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{d\tau}\|\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+z_{1}(z_{1}D_{1}-z_{2}D_{2})\lambda\|\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq 0,

which implies

(4.17)

\|\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(\tau)\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq\|\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\exp\bigl(-2z_{1}(z_{1}D_{1}-z_{2}D_{2})\lambda\tau\bigr).

While we get, by inserting
-\Delta\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}=\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}
into equation (
4.16
), that

(4.18)

\frac{1}{z_{1}(z_{1}D_{1}-z_{2}D_{2})}\partial_{\tau}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+c_{\scriptscriptstyle 1}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}=\nabla c_{\scriptscriptstyle 1}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)\cdot\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}.

Then by virtue of
c_{\scriptscriptstyle 1,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)\geq\lambda>0
and (
4.17
), we get, by multiplying equation (
4.18
) by
\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}
and integrating over
\Omega
, that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2z_{1}(z_{1}D_{1}-z_{2}D_{2})}

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\tau}\|\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\lambda\|\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{2}\lambda\|\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\lambda}\|\nabla c_{\scriptscriptstyle 1}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\exp\bigl(-2z_{1}(z_{1}D_{1}-z_{2}D_{2})\lambda\tau\bigr).

By using Gronwall’s inequality, one has

\|\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq\bigl(\|\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(0)\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{2\lambda^{2}}\|\nabla c_{\scriptscriptstyle 1}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\bigr)\exp\bigl(-z_{1}(z_{1}D_{1}-z_{2}D_{2})\lambda\tau\bigr).

While by taking spatial or time derivative of (
4.14
) and (
4.18
), we get along the same line, for
0\leq l\leq 3
and for some
M_{1},M_{2}
, that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{\tau}^{l}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|^{2}_{H^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq M_{1}\Bigl(\|\partial_{\tau}^{l}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(0)\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\|\nabla^{j}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{8-2j}\|\nabla\partial_{\tau}^{l}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j=1}^{2}\|\nabla^{j}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{6-2j}\|\partial_{\tau}^{l}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(0)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\nabla\partial_{\tau}^{l}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(0)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Bigr)e^{-M_{2}\tau}

\displaystyle\leq M_{1}\sum_{j=0}^{3}\|c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)\|_{H^{5}}^{2j}\|\partial_{\tau}^{l}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(0)\|^{2}_{H^{2}}e^{-M_{2}\tau}.

Observing that

\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}=\frac{z_{1}D_{1}-z_{2}D_{2}}{D_{1}}c_{\scriptscriptstyle 1,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}=\frac{z_{2}D_{2}-z_{1}D_{1}}{D_{2}}c_{\scriptscriptstyle 2,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}

and

\|\partial_{\tau}^{l+1}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(0)\|_{H^{2}}\leq M\|c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)\|_{H^{5}}\|\partial_{\tau}^{l}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(0)\|_{H^{2}},

we complete the proof of Proposition
4.1
.
∎

The next order of initial layer
(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(3)},\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},u_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(3)})
satisfy

(4.19)

\quad\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\partial_{\tau}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(3)}=z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}(0)\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}),\quad x\in\Omega,\tau>0,\\
-\Delta\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}=\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(3)},\\
\partial_{\tau}u_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(3)}+\nabla p_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}=0,\\
\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits u_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(3)}=0.\end{array}\right.

We implement the system (
4.19
) with the boundary conditions:

(4.20)

\begin{split}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(3)}(x^{\prime},y,\tau=0)=-\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(3)}(x^{\prime},y,t=0)=\Delta\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}(t=0),\\
(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(3)},\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},u_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(3)},p_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})(x^{\prime},y,\tau\rightarrow+\infty)=0\quad\hbox{and}\quad\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}|_{\partial\Omega}=0.\end{split}

Similarly one has
u_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(3)}=p_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}=0
and the following Proposition.

Proposition 4.2
.

Let
c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0),c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}(0)\in H^{5}
and
c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(3)}(0)\in H^{2}.
Then the system (
4.19
)-(
4.20
) admits a unique solution in
C^{\infty}([0,+\infty);H^{2})
.
Moreover, there exist positive constants
M_{1}
,
M_{2}
depending only on
\lambda,z_{i}
and
D_{i}
for
i=1,2
, such that for
0\leq l\leq 3
, we have

\begin{split}&\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|\partial_{\tau}^{l}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(3)}(\tau)\|_{H^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{\tau}^{l}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}(\tau)\|_{H^{4}}^{2}\\
&\qquad\qquad\leq M_{1}\bigl(\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)\|_{H^{5}}^{12}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}(0)\|_{H^{5}}^{12}+1\bigr)\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(3)}(0)\|_{H^{2}}^{2}e^{-M_{2}\tau}.\end{split}

If we assume in addition that
\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\equiv 0
and
\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(0)=0
, then we have
\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\equiv 0
.

\bullet
Analysis of mixed layer solutions.

For mixed boundary layer, we also only deal with the one near
y=0
. Firstly by comparing the leading order, one has
\partial_{\xi}p_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(-2)}=0=p_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(-2)}(x^{\prime},\xi\rightarrow+\infty)
, which leads to
p_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(-2)}=0
. Thus the next order
(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},v_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},w_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},p_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(-1)})
satisfy

(4.21)

\quad\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\partial_{\tau}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}=D_{i}\partial_{\xi}^{2}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+z_{i}D_{i}\partial_{\xi}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\partial_{\xi}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\\
\qquad\qquad\qquad+z_{i}D_{i}\gamma_{i}(x^{\prime},0,0)\partial_{\xi}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},\qquad 0<\xi,\tau<+\infty,\\
-\partial_{\xi}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}=\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},\\
\partial_{\tau}v_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}-\nu\partial_{\xi}^{2}v_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}=0,\\
\partial_{\tau}w_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}-\nu\partial_{\xi}^{2}w_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\partial_{\xi}p_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(-1)}=0,\\
\partial_{\xi}w_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}=0.\end{array}\right.

We implement the system (
4.21
) with the boundary conditions:

\displaystyle(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},v_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},w_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)})(x^{\prime},\xi=0,\tau)=-(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},v_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},w_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)})(x^{\prime},y=0,\tau)=0,

\displaystyle(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},v_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},w_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},p_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(-1)})(x^{\prime},\xi\rightarrow+\infty,\tau)=0,

\displaystyle(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},v_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},w_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},p_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(-1)})(x^{\prime},\xi,\tau\rightarrow+\infty)=0.

Thus one has
(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},v_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},w_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},p_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(-1)})=0
. By plugging them into the equation of next order, we obtain

(4.22)

\quad\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\partial_{\tau}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}=D_{i}\partial_{\xi}^{2}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+z_{i}D_{i}\gamma_{i}(x^{\prime},0,0)\partial_{\xi}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},\quad 0<\xi,\tau<+\infty,\\
-\partial_{\xi}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}=\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},\\
\partial_{\tau}v_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}-\nu\partial_{\xi}^{2}v_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}=0,\\
\partial_{\tau}w_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}-\nu\partial_{\xi}^{2}w_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\partial_{\xi}p_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}=0,\\
\partial_{\xi}w_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}=0.\end{array}\right.

We implement the system (
4.22
) with the boundary conditions:

\displaystyle(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},v_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},w_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})(x^{\prime},\xi=0,\tau)=-(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},v_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},w_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})(x^{\prime},y=0,\tau)=0,

\displaystyle(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},v_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},w_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},p_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)})(x^{\prime},\xi\rightarrow+\infty,\tau)=0,

\displaystyle(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},v_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},w_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},p_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)})(x^{\prime},\xi,\tau\rightarrow+\infty)=0.

Thus one has
(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},v_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},w_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},p_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)})=0
. By plugging them into the equation of next order, we obtain

(4.23)

\quad\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\partial_{\tau}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}=D_{i}\partial_{\xi}^{2}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+z_{i}D_{i}\gamma_{i}(x^{\prime},0,0)\partial_{\xi}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},\quad 0<\xi,\tau<+\infty,\\
-\partial_{\xi}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}=\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},\\
\partial_{\tau}v_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}-\nu\partial_{\xi}^{2}v_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}=0,\\
\partial_{\tau}w_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}-\nu\partial_{\xi}^{2}w_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\partial_{\xi}p_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}=0,\\
\partial_{\xi}w_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}=0.\end{array}\right.

We implement the system (
4.23
) with

(4.24)

\begin{split}&c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},\xi=0,\tau)=-c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},y=0,\tau),\\
&(v_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},w_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})(x^{\prime},\xi=0,\tau)=-(v_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},w_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})(x^{\prime},y=0,\tau)=0,\\
&(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},\partial_{\xi}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},v_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},w_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},p_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})(x^{\prime},\xi\rightarrow+\infty,\tau)=0,\\
&(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},v_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},w_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},p_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})(x^{\prime},\xi,\tau\rightarrow+\infty)=0.\end{split}

Similarly for
(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(k)},\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(k)},v_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(k)},w_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(k)},p_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(k)})
, one has

(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},v_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},w_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)},p_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(-1)})=0,

(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},v_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},w_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)},p_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)})=0,

and

(4.25)

\quad\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}\partial_{\tau}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}=D_{i}\partial_{\eta}^{2}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+z_{i}D_{i}\gamma_{i}(x^{\prime},1,0)\partial_{\eta}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},\quad 0<\eta,\tau<+\infty,\\
-\partial_{\eta}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}=\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},\\
\partial_{\tau}v_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}-\nu\partial_{\eta}^{2}v_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}=0,\\
\partial_{\tau}w_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}-\nu\partial_{\eta}^{2}w_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}-\partial_{\eta}p_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}=0,\\
-\partial_{\eta}w_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}=0.\end{array}\right.

with boundary conditions:

(4.26)

\begin{split}&c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},\eta=0,\tau)=-c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},y=1,\tau),\\
&(v_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},w_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})(x^{\prime},\eta=0,\tau)=-(v_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},w_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})(x^{\prime},y=1,\tau)=0,\\
&(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},\partial_{\eta}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},v_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},w_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},p_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})(x^{\prime},\eta\rightarrow+\infty,\tau)=0,\\
&(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},v_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},w_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},p_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})(x^{\prime},\eta,\tau\rightarrow+\infty)=0.\end{split}

The next Proposition is concerned with the time and spatial decay of
c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}
, the same results also hold for
c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}
.

Proposition 4.3
.

Let
c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)\in H^{5}
,
c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(0)\in H^{2}
and
\nabla^{k}_{x^{\prime}}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},\cdot,0)
belong to
H^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{+}_{\xi},\langle\xi\rangle^{4}\,d\xi)
for any
x^{\prime}\in\mathbb{T}^{d-1}
and
0\leq k\leq 2
, then system (
4.23
)-(
4.24
) admits a unique solution in
C([0,+\infty);H^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{+}_{\xi},\langle\xi\rangle^{4}\,d\xi))
. Moreover, there exists
M
depending only on
\lambda,\Lambda,\gamma_{i}(x),z_{i}
and
D_{i}
for
i=1,2
, such that for
0\leq k\leq 2
and
1\leq l\leq 2
, we have

(4.27)

\begin{split}&\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|\nabla^{k}_{x^{\prime}}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{H^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{+}_{\xi},\langle\xi\rangle^{4}\,d\xi)}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\|\nabla^{k}_{x^{\prime}}\partial_{\xi}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{H^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{+}_{\xi},\langle\xi\rangle^{4}\,d\xi)}\,d\tau\\
&\quad+\int_{0}^{+\infty}\|\nabla^{k}_{x^{\prime}}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},\cdot,\tau)\|^{2}_{H^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{+}_{\xi},\langle\xi\rangle^{4}\,d\xi)}\,d\tau\leq\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|\nabla^{k}_{x^{\prime}}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},\cdot,0)\|^{2}_{H^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{+}_{\xi},\langle\xi\rangle^{4}\,d\xi)}\\
&\qquad+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{3}\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\nabla^{k}_{x^{\prime}}\partial_{\tau}^{j}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},0,\tau)|^{2}\,d\tau+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{2}\sup_{\tau}|\nabla^{k}_{x^{\prime}}\partial_{\tau}^{j}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},0,\tau)|^{2}\end{split}

and

(4.28)

\begin{split}&\|\nabla^{k}_{x^{\prime}}\partial_{\xi}\partial_{\tau}^{l}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{\xi}}+\|\nabla^{k}_{x^{\prime}}\partial_{\xi}\partial_{\tau}^{l}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\\
&\qquad\lesssim\|\nabla^{k}_{x^{\prime}}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},\cdot,\tau)\|_{H^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{+}_{\xi},\langle\xi\rangle^{4}\,d\xi)}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|\nabla^{k}_{x^{\prime}}\partial_{\xi}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},\cdot,\tau)\|_{H^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{+}_{\xi},\langle\xi\rangle^{4}\,d\xi)}.\end{split}

Proof.

Once again, we shall only deal with the
a priori
estimate in weighted Sobolev space
H^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{+}_{\xi},\langle\xi\rangle^{4}\,d\xi)
. For simplicity, we omit
x^{\prime}
, and denote
\gamma_{i}(x^{\prime},0,0)
by
\gamma_{i}
throughout this proof. By introducing

(4.29)

\quad\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}a_{i}(\tau)\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}-c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(y=0,\tau),\quad r(\tau)\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}z_{1}a_{1}+z_{2}a_{2},\\
\alpha_{i}\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}-a_{i}(\tau)e^{-\xi},\quad\omega\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}-r(\tau)e^{-\xi}=z_{1}\alpha_{1}+z_{2}\alpha_{2},\end{array}\right.

we consider an equivalent system:

(4.30)

\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}-D_{i}\partial_{\xi}^{2}\alpha_{i}+z_{i}D_{i}\gamma_{i}\omega=-(\frac{da_{i}}{d\tau}-D_{i}a_{i}+z_{i}D_{i}\gamma_{i}r)e^{-\xi},\quad 0<\xi,\tau<+\infty,

implemented with boundary conditions

\alpha_{i}(0,\tau)=\omega(0,\tau)=0.

Firstly, we get, by taking
L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+}_{\xi})
inner product of the equation (
4.30
) with
\frac{\alpha_{i}}{\gamma_{i}D_{i}}
,
and then summing up the resulting equalities for
i=1,2
, that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\gamma_{i}D_{i}}\frac{d}{d\tau}\|\alpha_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{1}{\gamma_{i}}\|\partial_{\xi}\alpha_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(|a_{i}|+|\frac{da_{i}}{d\tau}|\bigr)\int_{0}^{+\infty}e^{-\xi}\alpha_{i}d\xi

\displaystyle\leq M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(|a_{i}|^{2}+|\frac{da_{i}}{d\tau}|^{2}\bigr)+\frac{1}{2\Lambda}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|\partial_{\xi}\alpha_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2},

where in the last step we used the following equality:

\int_{0}^{+\infty}e^{-\xi}fd\xi=-\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{d}{d\xi}e^{-\xi}fd\xi=\int_{0}^{+\infty}e^{-\xi}f^{\prime}d\xi,\quad\textit{for any}\ f\in C^{1}_{\xi}\cap L^{\infty}_{\xi}\ \textit{with}\ f(0)=0.

As a consequence, we obtain

(4.31)

\begin{split}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{1}{2\gamma_{i}D_{i}}&\|\alpha_{i}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{1}{2\gamma_{i}}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\|\partial_{\xi}\alpha_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}\,d\tau+\int_{0}^{+\infty}\|\omega\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}\,d\tau\\
&\leq\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{1}{2\gamma_{i}D_{i}}\|\alpha_{i}(0)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\bigl(|a_{i}|^{2}+|\frac{da_{i}}{d\tau}|^{2}\bigr)\,d\tau.\end{split}

While by taking
L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+}_{\xi})
inner product of the equation (
4.30
) with
-\frac{\partial_{\xi}^{2}\alpha_{i}}{\gamma_{i}D_{i}}
and
then summarizing the resulting equalities for
i=1,2
, one has

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\gamma_{i}D_{i}}\frac{d}{d\tau}\|\partial_{\xi}\alpha_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{1}{\gamma_{i}}\|\partial_{\xi}^{2}\alpha_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}+\|\partial_{\xi}\omega\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(|a_{i}|^{2}+|\frac{da_{i}}{d\tau}|^{2}\bigr)+\frac{1}{2\Lambda}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|\partial_{\xi}^{2}\alpha_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2},

which implies

(4.32)

\begin{split}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{1}{2\gamma_{i}D_{i}}&\|\partial_{\xi}\alpha_{i}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{1}{2\gamma_{i}}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\|\partial_{\xi}^{2}\alpha_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}\,d\tau+\int_{0}^{+\infty}\|\partial_{\xi}\omega\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}\,d\tau\\
&\leq\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{1}{2\gamma_{i}D_{i}}\|\partial_{\xi}\alpha_{i}(0)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\bigl(|a_{i}|^{2}+|\frac{da_{i}}{d\tau}|^{2}\bigr)\,d\tau.\end{split}

Next we consider higher derivative estimate of
\alpha_{i}.
We first get, by taking time derivative of (
4.30
), that

(4.33)

\partial_{\tau}^{2}\alpha_{i}-D_{i}\partial_{\xi}^{2}\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}+z_{i}D_{i}\gamma_{i}\partial_{\tau}\omega=-(\frac{d^{2}a_{i}}{d\tau^{2}}-D_{i}\frac{da_{i}}{d\tau}+z_{i}D_{i}\gamma_{i}\frac{dr}{d\tau})e^{-\xi},

implemented with boundary conditions

\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}(0,\tau)=\partial_{\tau}\omega(0,\tau)=0.

Along the same line to the derivation of (
4.31
) and (
4.32
), one has, for
k=0,1

(4.34)

\begin{split}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{1}{2\gamma_{i}D_{i}}&\|\partial_{\xi}^{k}\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{1}{2\gamma_{i}}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\|\partial_{\xi}^{k+1}\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}\,d\tau+\int_{0}^{+\infty}\|\partial_{\xi}^{k}\partial_{\tau}\omega\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}\,d\tau\\
&\leq\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{1}{2\gamma_{i}D_{i}}\|\partial_{\xi}^{k}\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}(0)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\bigl(|\frac{da_{i}}{d\tau}|^{2}+|\frac{d^{2}a_{i}}{d\tau^{2}}|^{2}\bigr)\,d\tau,\end{split}

Now in view of (
4.29
), (
4.31
), (
4.32
), (
4.34
) and

\|\partial_{\xi}^{k}\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}(0)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}\lesssim\|\alpha_{i}(0)\|_{H^{k+2}_{\xi}}+M(|a_{i}(0)|+|\frac{da_{i}}{d\tau}(0)|),

we get, for
0\leq k,l\leq 1
,

\begin{split}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\Bigl(\frac{1}{2\gamma_{i}D_{i}}&\|\partial_{\xi}^{k}\partial_{\tau}^{l}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2\gamma_{i}}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\|\partial_{\xi}^{k+1}\partial_{\tau}^{l}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}\,d\tau\Bigr)+\int_{0}^{+\infty}\|\partial_{\xi}^{k}\partial_{\tau}^{l}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}\,d\tau\\
&\leq M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(0)\|_{H^{3}_{\xi}}^{2}+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\frac{d^{j}a_{i}}{d\tau^{j}}|^{2}\,d\tau+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{1}\sup_{\tau}|\frac{d^{j}a_{i}}{d\tau^{j}}(\tau)|^{2}.\end{split}

On the other hand, by taking
L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+}_{\xi})
inner product of
the equation (
4.30
) with
\xi^{2}\frac{\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}}{\gamma_{i}D_{i}}
and integrating by parts, and then summarizing the equalities for
i=1,2
, we find

\begin{split}&\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{1}{2\gamma_{i}}\frac{d}{d\tau}\|\xi\partial_{\xi}\alpha_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{d\tau}\|\xi\omega\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{1}{\gamma_{i}D_{i}}\|\xi\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}\\
&\leq M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(|a_{i}|+|\frac{da_{i}}{d\tau}|\bigr)\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}e^{-\xi}\xi^{2}|\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}|d\xi+M\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\xi\partial_{\xi}\alpha_{i}\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}|\,d\xi\\
&\leq M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(|a_{i}|^{2}+|\frac{da_{i}}{d\tau}|^{2}+\|\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}+\|\partial_{\xi}\alpha_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}\bigr)+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{1}{2\max_{i}\gamma_{i}D_{i}}\|\xi\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2},\end{split}

which together with (
4.30
) implies

(4.35)

\begin{split}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{1}{2\gamma_{i}}&\|\xi\partial_{\xi}\alpha_{i}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|\xi\omega(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{1}{2\gamma_{i}D_{i}}\int_{0}^{\infty}\|\xi\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}\,d\tau\\
&\leq\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{1}{2\gamma_{i}}\|\xi\partial_{\xi}\alpha_{i}(0)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|\xi\omega(0)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}\\
&\quad+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\bigl(|a_{i}|^{2}+|\frac{da_{i}}{d\tau}|^{2}+\|\partial_{\xi}\alpha_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}+\|\partial_{\xi}^{2}\alpha_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}+\|\omega\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}\bigr)\,d\tau.\end{split}

Similarly, by taking
L^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+}_{\xi})
inner product of the equation (
4.33
) with
\xi^{4}\frac{\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}}{\gamma_{i}D_{i}}
and integrating by parts, and then summarizing the equalities for
i=1,2
, we obtain

\begin{split}&\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{1}{2\gamma_{i}D_{i}}\frac{d}{d\tau}\|\xi^{2}\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{1}{\gamma_{i}}\|\xi^{2}\partial_{\xi}\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}+\|\xi^{2}\partial_{\tau}\omega\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}\\
&\leq M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(|\frac{da_{i}}{d\tau}|+|\frac{d^{2}a_{i}}{d\tau^{2}}|\bigr)\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}e^{-\xi}\xi^{4}|\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}|d\xi+M\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\xi^{2}\partial_{\xi}\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}\xi\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}|\,d\xi\\
&\leq M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(|\frac{da_{i}}{d\tau}|^{2}+|\frac{d^{2}a_{i}}{d\tau^{2}}|^{2}+\|\xi\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}\bigr)+\frac{1}{2\Lambda}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|\xi^{2}\partial_{\xi}\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}.\end{split}

Thus in view of (
4.31
), (
4.32
) and (
4.35
), to sum up, we get

\begin{split}\sum_{i=1}^{2}&\frac{1}{2\gamma_{i}D_{i}}\|\xi^{2}\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{1}{2\gamma_{i}}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\|\xi^{2}\partial_{\xi}\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}\,d\tau+\int_{0}^{+\infty}\|\xi^{2}\partial_{\tau}\omega\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}\,d\tau\\
&\leq\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{1}{2\gamma_{i}D_{i}}\|\xi^{2}\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}(0)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\bigl(|\frac{da_{i}}{d\tau}|^{2}+|\frac{d^{2}a_{i}}{d\tau^{2}}|^{2}+\|\xi\partial_{\tau}\alpha_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}\bigr)\,d\tau\\
&\leq M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|\alpha_{i}(0)\|^{2}_{H^{2}(\mathbb{R}^{+}_{\xi},\langle\xi\rangle^{4}\,d\xi)}+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{2}\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\frac{d^{j}a_{i}}{d\tau^{j}}|^{2}\,d\tau+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{1}\sup_{\tau}|\frac{d^{j}a_{i}}{d\tau^{j}}(\tau)|^{2}.\end{split}

Along the same line, one has the estimate of derivative of the highest order

\begin{split}\sum_{i=1}^{2}&\frac{1}{2\gamma_{i}D_{i}}\|\xi^{2}\partial_{\tau}^{2}\alpha_{i}(\tau)\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{1}{2\gamma_{i}}\int_{0}^{+\infty}\|\xi^{2}\partial_{\xi}\partial_{\tau}^{2}\alpha_{i}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}\,d\tau+\int_{0}^{+\infty}\|\xi^{2}\partial_{\tau}^{2}\omega\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}^{2}\,d\tau\\
&\leq M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|\alpha_{i}(0)\|^{2}_{H^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{+}_{\xi},\langle\xi\rangle^{4}\,d\xi)}+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{3}\int_{0}^{+\infty}|\frac{d^{j}a_{i}}{d\tau^{j}}|^{2}\,d\tau+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\sum_{j=0}^{2}\sup_{\tau}|\frac{d^{j}a_{i}}{d\tau^{j}}(\tau)|^{2}.\end{split}

By taking derivatives in direction
x^{\prime}
, we are able to finish the proof of
(\ref {estimate of cilmr})
by the same argument. Lastly, (
4.28
) is a direct consequence of the equation of
\partial_{\tau}^{l}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}
and the following inequality

\displaystyle|\nabla^{k}_{x^{\prime}}\partial_{\xi}\partial_{\tau}^{l}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}|=|\int_{\xi}^{+\infty}\langle z\rangle^{-2}\langle z\rangle^{2}\nabla^{k}_{x^{\prime}}\partial_{\tau}^{l}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}dz|\lesssim\langle\xi\rangle^{-\frac{3}{2}}\|\langle\xi\rangle^{2}\nabla^{k}_{x^{\prime}}\partial_{\tau}^{l}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}_{\xi}}.

This competes the proof of Proposition
4.3
.
∎

5.
The Convergence in
L^{\infty}_{T}(H^{2})

In this section, we focus on estimating the difference between the solutions of (
1.4
) and the expansions in
L^{\infty}_{T}(H^{2})
. To this end, we
shall construct approximate solutions (
c_{i}^{app},\Phi^{app},u^{app}
) with the same boundary conditions as
(c^{\varepsilon}_{i},\Phi^{\varepsilon},u^{\varepsilon})
, and denote

(c_{i}^{S},\Phi^{S},u^{S})\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}(c^{\varepsilon}_{i}-c_{i}^{app},\Phi^{\varepsilon}-\Phi^{app},u^{\varepsilon}-u^{app})
so that

(5.1)

\quad\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}c_{i}^{S}(x^{\prime},y,t)=c^{\varepsilon}_{i}(x^{\prime},y,t)-c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(x^{\prime},y,t)-\varepsilon c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}(x^{\prime},y,t)-\varepsilon^{2}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},y,t)\\
\qquad\qquad\qquad-\varepsilon^{2}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},y,\tau)-\varepsilon^{2}f(y)c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},\xi,t)-\varepsilon^{2}g(y)c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},\eta,t)\\
\qquad\qquad\qquad-\varepsilon^{2}f(y)c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},\xi,t)-\varepsilon^{2}g(y)c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},\eta,t),\\
\Phi^{S}(x^{\prime},y,t)=\Phi^{\varepsilon}(x^{\prime},y,t)-\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(x^{\prime},y,t)-\varepsilon\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}(x^{\prime},y,t)-\varepsilon^{2}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},y,t)\\
\qquad\qquad\qquad-\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(x^{\prime},y,\tau)-\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}(x^{\prime},y^{\prime},\tau)\\
\qquad\qquad\qquad-\varepsilon^{2}f(y)\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},\xi,t)-\varepsilon^{2}g(y)\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},\eta,t),\\
u^{S}(x^{\prime},y,t)=u^{\varepsilon}(x^{\prime},y,t)-u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(x^{\prime},y,t)-\varepsilon u^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}(x^{\prime},y,t),\end{array}\right.

implemented with the boundary conditions:

(5.2)

c_{i}^{S}|_{\partial\Omega}=0,\quad\Phi^{S}|_{\partial\Omega}=0\quad\hbox{and}\quad u^{S}|_{\partial\Omega}=0.

Due to (
5.2
), we have
\|c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|\nabla c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|\Delta c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}}
, and similar results hold for
\Phi^{S}
and
u^{S}
. We shall first handle the estimates of
\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}(L^{2})}
and
\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}(L^{2})}
, and then use the
c_{i}^{S}
equation to derive the estimate of
\|\Delta c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}(L^{2})}
.

5.1.
Estimate of
\|(\nabla c_{i}^{S},\varepsilon\Delta\Phi^{S},\nabla u^{S})\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}(L^{2})}
.

The goal of this subsection is to prove the following type of inequality:

(5.3)

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\bigl(\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\varepsilon^{2}\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|u^{S}\|^{2}_{V}\bigr)+\int_{\Omega}\bigl(\frac{|\Delta c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\Delta\Phi^{S}|^{2}\bigr)\,dx

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{-2}\|\nabla\rho^{S}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\nu\|Au^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq O(\varepsilon^{3}).

To this end, we write the equations of
(c_{i}^{S},\Phi^{S},u^{S})
as

(5.4a)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}c_{i}^{S}+u^{S}\cdot\nabla c_{i}^{S}=D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits\bigl(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(\frac{\nabla c_{i}^{S}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\nabla\Phi^{S})\bigr)+z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{S}\nabla\Phi^{S})+K_{i}+L_{i}+M_{i},

(5.4b)

\displaystyle\rho^{S}=-\varepsilon^{2}\Delta\Phi^{S}+N,

(5.4c)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}u^{S}+u^{S}\cdot\nabla u^{S}-\nu\Delta u^{S}-\nabla p^{S}=-\rho^{S}\nabla\Phi^{S}+O+P,

(5.4d)

\displaystyle\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits u^{S}=0,

where

\displaystyle K_{i}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}-(u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon u^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})\cdot\nabla c_{i}^{S}

\displaystyle-u^{S}\cdot\nabla(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})

\displaystyle+z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits\bigl(c_{i}^{S}\nabla(\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}f\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}g\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\bigr)

\displaystyle+z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits\bigl((\varepsilon c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\nabla\Phi^{S}\bigr),

\displaystyle L_{i}

\displaystyle\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}z_{i}D_{i}\bigl(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}-\gamma_{i}(x^{\prime},0,t)\bigr)f\partial_{\xi}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+z_{i}D_{i}\bigl(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}-\gamma_{i}(x^{\prime},1,t)\bigr)f\partial_{\eta}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}

\displaystyle\quad+z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits\bigl((c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}-c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0))\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\bigr),

\displaystyle M_{i}\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}-\varepsilon^{3}u^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\cdot\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}u^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\cdot\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}

\displaystyle-(u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon u^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})\cdot\nabla(\varepsilon^{2}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{2}D_{i}\Delta c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}D_{i}(f\Delta_{x^{\prime}}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\Delta_{x^{\prime}}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\Delta_{x^{\prime}}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\Delta_{x^{\prime}}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{2}D_{i}(f^{\prime\prime}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g^{\prime\prime}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f^{\prime\prime}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g^{\prime\prime}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})

\displaystyle+2\varepsilon D_{i}(f^{\prime}\partial_{\xi}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}-g^{\prime}\partial_{\eta}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f^{\prime}\partial_{\xi}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}-g^{\prime}\partial_{\eta}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{2}z_{i}D_{i}\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\nabla(f\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})+\varepsilon^{2}z_{i}D_{i}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(f^{\prime\prime}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g^{\prime\prime}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\Delta_{x^{\prime}}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\Delta_{x^{\prime}}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})

\displaystyle+2\varepsilon z_{i}D_{i}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(f^{\prime}\partial_{\xi}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}-g^{\prime}\partial_{\eta}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{3}z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\nabla\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\nabla\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})+\varepsilon^{4}z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\nabla\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})+\varepsilon z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})

\displaystyle+z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits\bigl((\varepsilon c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\nabla(\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}f\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}g\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\bigr)

\displaystyle+z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits\bigl((\varepsilon^{2}fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\nabla(\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}f\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}g\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\bigr)

\displaystyle+z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits\bigl((\varepsilon^{2}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\nabla(\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\bigr)

\displaystyle-\varepsilon^{2}f\partial_{t}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}-\varepsilon^{2}g\partial_{t}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}-z_{i}D_{i}\gamma_{i}(x^{\prime},0,0)f\partial_{\xi}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}-z_{i}D_{i}\gamma_{i}(x^{\prime},1,0)g\partial_{\eta}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},

\displaystyle N\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}

\displaystyle-\varepsilon^{3}\Delta\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}-\varepsilon^{4}\Delta\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}-\varepsilon^{3}\Delta\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}-2\varepsilon^{3}(f^{\prime}\partial_{\xi}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}-g^{\prime}\partial_{\eta}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})

\displaystyle-\varepsilon^{4}(f\Delta_{x^{\prime}}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\Delta_{x^{\prime}}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f^{\prime\prime}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g^{\prime\prime}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})+\varepsilon^{2}(f\partial_{\xi}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\partial_{\eta}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}),

\displaystyle O\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}

\displaystyle-u^{S}\cdot\nabla(u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon u^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})-(u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon u^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})\cdot\nabla u^{S}

\displaystyle-\varepsilon^{2}(\rho^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\nabla\Phi^{S}

\displaystyle-\rho^{S}\nabla(\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}f\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}g\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}),

and

\displaystyle P\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}

\displaystyle-\varepsilon^{2}u^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\cdot\nabla u^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}-\varepsilon^{2}(\rho^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})

\displaystyle\qquad\cdot\nabla(\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}f\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}g\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}).

Theorem 5.1
.

Let
d=2
or
3
, we assume that the initial data is “well-prepared” i.e.
\rho^{\varepsilon}(0)=0
,
\lambda\leq c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)
,
\bigl(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0),c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}(0),c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(0),u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0),u^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}(0),u^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(0)\bigr)\in H^{5}
,
\bigl(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(0),c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(3)}(0)\bigr)\in H^{2}
and
\nabla^{k}_{x^{\prime}}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},\cdot,0)
belong to
H^{4}(\mathbb{R}^{+}_{\xi},\langle\xi\rangle^{4}\,d\xi)
for any
x^{\prime}\in\mathbb{T}^{d-1}
and
0\leq k\leq 2
. Let
(c^{\varepsilon}_{i},u^{\varepsilon},\psi^{\varepsilon})\in C([0,T];H^{5})
be the strong solution of system (
1.4
)-(
1.5
) with initial data
\bigl(c^{\varepsilon}_{i}(0),u^{\varepsilon}(0)\bigr)
, where
T\leq T_{0}
for
T_{0}

being determined by Proposition
4.1
. If there exists a positive constant
M_{1}>0
so that

(5.5)

\displaystyle\|\nabla c_{i}^{S}(0)\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla u^{S}(0)\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon\|\Delta\Phi^{S}(0)\|_{L^{2}}\leq M_{1}\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}.

Then for
\varepsilon
being sufficiently small, there exists a positive constant
M_{2}
depending only on initial data,
\nu,M_{1},T,\lambda,\Lambda,W(x),\gamma_{i}(x),z_{i}
and
D_{i}
for
i=1,2
, so that

(5.6a)

\displaystyle\|\nabla c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}(L^{2})}+\|\nabla u^{S}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}(L^{2})}+\varepsilon\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}(L^{2})}\leq M_{2}\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}},

(5.6b)

\displaystyle\|\Delta c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}_{T}(L^{2})}+\|Au^{S}\|_{L^{2}_{T}(L^{2})}+\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}_{T}(L^{2})}+\varepsilon^{-1}\|\nabla\rho^{S}\|_{L^{2}_{T}(L^{2})}\leq M_{2}\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}.

Lemma 5.1
.

Under the assumptions in Theorem
5.1
, for
\varepsilon\leq 1
, one has

(5.7)

\begin{split}&\|K_{i}\|_{L^{2}}+\|O_{i}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\Bigl(\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx\Bigr)^{\frac{1}{2}}+\|u^{S}\|_{V}+\varepsilon\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}},\\
&\|L_{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{2})}+\|M_{i}\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{2})}\lesssim\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}},\\
&\|L_{i}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2})}+\|M_{i}\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2})}\lesssim\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}},\\
&\|N\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2})}\lesssim\varepsilon^{3},\quad\|\nabla N\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2})}\lesssim\varepsilon^{\frac{5}{2}},\quad\|\partial_{t}N\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2})}\lesssim\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}},\\
&\|P\|_{L^{\infty}(0,T;L^{2})}+\|P\|_{L^{2}(0,T;L^{2})}\lesssim\varepsilon^{2}.\\
.\end{split}

The proof of Lemma
5.1
involves tedious calculation, we
leave it in the Appendix
A
.

With Lemma
5.1
, we are able to refine the convergence rate of
\|(\nabla c_{i}^{S},\varepsilon\Delta\Phi^{S},\nabla u^{S})\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}(L^{2})}
, which will be crucial for the proof of Theorem
5.1
. In order to do so, let us define

T^{\star}\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}\sup\bigl\{\ t\leq T;\quad\|\nabla c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}(L^{2})}\leq 1,\ \|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}(L^{2})}\leq 1,\ \|\nabla u^{S}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}(L^{2})}\leq 1\ \bigr\}.

Lemma 5.2
.

Under the assumptions in Theorem
5.1
, for
\varepsilon
being sufficiently small, there exists a constant
M
depending only on initial data,
\nu,M_{1},T,\lambda,\Lambda,z_{i}
and
D_{i}
for
i=1,2
, so that

(5.8a)

\displaystyle\|c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T^{\star}}(L^{2})}^{2}+\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T^{\star}}(L^{2})}^{2}+\|u^{S}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T^{\star}}(L^{2})}^{2}\leq M\varepsilon^{3},

(5.8b)

\displaystyle\|\nabla c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}_{T^{\star}}(L^{2})}^{2}+\|\nabla\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}_{T^{\star}}(L^{2})}^{2}+\varepsilon^{2}\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}_{T^{\star}}(L^{2})}^{2}+\|\nabla u^{S}\|_{L^{2}_{T^{\star}}(L^{2})}^{2}\leq M\varepsilon^{3}.

Proof.

Thanks to (
5.4b
), we get, by taking
L^{2}
inner product of
\partial_{t}c_{i}^{S}+u^{S}\cdot\nabla c_{i}^{S}
with
\frac{c_{i}^{S}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\Phi^{S}
and using integration by parts and then summarizing the resulting equalities for
i=1,2
, that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}(\partial_{t}c_{i}^{S}+u^{S}\cdot\nabla c_{i}^{S})(\frac{c_{i}^{S}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\Phi^{S})\,dx

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Omega}\frac{(c_{i}^{S})^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx-\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}(c_{i}^{S})^{2}\frac{d}{dt}(\frac{1}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}})\,dx+\int_{\Omega}\frac{u^{S}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\nabla c_{i}^{S}c_{i}^{S}\,dx

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\nabla\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}N\Phi^{S}\,dx-\int_{\Omega}u^{S}\cdot\nabla\Phi^{S}\rho^{S}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad\geq\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Omega}\frac{(c_{i}^{S})^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\nabla\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-\frac{D^{*}}{16}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\Bigl(\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+z_{i}^{2}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\nabla\Phi^{S}|^{2}\,dx\Bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{\nu}{2}\|u\|_{V}^{2}-M\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{\Omega}\frac{(c_{i}^{S})^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx-M\|\nabla c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{4}\|u^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad-\int_{\Omega}u^{S}\cdot\nabla\Phi^{S}\rho^{S}\,dx-M\|\partial_{t}N\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Observing that
\Lambda\geq c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\geq\lambda>0
, we find

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}D_{i}\int_{\Omega}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits\bigl(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(\frac{\nabla c_{i}^{S}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\nabla\Phi^{S})\bigr)(\frac{c_{i}^{S}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\Phi^{S})\,dx

\displaystyle\quad=-\sum_{i=1}^{2}D_{i}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(\frac{\nabla c_{i}^{S}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\nabla\Phi^{S})(\frac{\nabla c_{i}^{S}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\nabla\Phi^{S}+c_{i}^{S}\nabla(\frac{1}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}))\,dx

\displaystyle\quad\leq-\frac{D^{*}}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\Bigl(\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+z_{i}^{2}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\nabla\Phi^{S}|^{2}\,dx\Bigr)-\varepsilon^{2}D^{*}\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+M\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{(c_{i}^{S})^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\varepsilon^{-2}\|N\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Next we estimate the quadratic term. Indeed by using integration by parts and Sobolev’s embedding, one has

\begin{split}&\sum_{i=1}^{2}D_{i}\int_{\Omega}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{S}\nabla\Phi^{S})(\frac{c_{i}^{S}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\Phi^{S})\,dx\\
&\quad\leq M(\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}+1)\|c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}(\|\nabla c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}})\\
&\quad\leq M(\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{4}+1)\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{4}\|c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+\frac{D^{*}}{16}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\Bigl(\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+z_{i}^{2}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\nabla\Phi^{S}|^{2}\,dx\Bigr).\end{split}

By using integration by parts and Hölder’s inequality, one has

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}K_{i}(\frac{c_{i}^{S}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\Phi^{S})\,dx\leq\frac{D^{*}}{32}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\Bigl(\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+z_{i}^{2}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\nabla\Phi^{S}|^{2}\,dx\Bigr)

\displaystyle\quad+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}(1+\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2})\Bigl(\|u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon u^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{(c_{i}^{S})^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad+\|c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}\|u^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\nabla(\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}f\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}g\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{(c_{i}^{S})^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{2}\|c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\nabla\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Bigr).

The estimates of rest terms are straightforward

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}(L_{i}+M_{i})(\frac{c_{i}^{S}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\Phi^{S})\,dx

\displaystyle\leq\frac{D^{*}}{32}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\Bigl(\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+z_{i}^{2}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\nabla\Phi^{S}|^{2}\,dx\Bigr)

\displaystyle\quad+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}(\|L_{i}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|M_{i}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}).

Whereas by multiplying (
5.4c
) by
u^{S}
and using integration by parts, we get

\begin{split}\frac{1}{2}&\frac{d}{dt}\|u^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\nu\|\nabla u^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
&\leq\bigl(1+\|\nabla(u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon u^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})\|_{L^{\infty}}+\varepsilon^{4}\|\rho^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\\
&\qquad+\|(\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}f\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}g\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\bigr)\|u^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
&\quad-\int_{\Omega}u^{S}\cdot\nabla\Phi^{S}\rho^{S}\,dx+\|P\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{D^{*}}{16}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+z_{i}^{2}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\nabla\Phi^{S}|^{2}\,dx\bigr).\\
\end{split}

By summing up all the above inequalities and using (
1.13a
-
1.13c
) and the estimates of the layer solutions obtained in Section
4
, we achieve

(5.9)

\begin{split}\frac{1}{2}&\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Omega}\frac{(c_{i}^{S})^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\nabla\Phi^{S}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|u^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\nu}{2}\|\nabla u^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{D^{*}\varepsilon^{2}}{2}\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\\
&+\frac{D^{*}}{4}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+z^{2}_{i}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\nabla\Phi^{S}|^{2}\,dx\bigr)+D^{*}\varepsilon^{2}\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\\
&\leq\kappa_{1}(t)\Bigl(\int_{\Omega}\frac{(c_{i}^{S})^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|u^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Bigr)+\kappa_{2}(t)\qquad\text{on}\ [0,T^{\star}],\end{split}

provided that

\varepsilon<1
is so small that
M_{1}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\frac{1}{2}
and consequently
0<T^{\star}
,
where
\kappa_{1}
and
\kappa_{2}
are positive functions satisfying: for some
M
depending only on initial data,
\nu,M_{1},T,\lambda,\Lambda,W(x),

\gamma_{i}(x),z_{i}
and
D_{i}
for
i=1,2
, there hold

\int_{0}^{T^{\star}}\kappa_{1}(t^{\prime})\,dt^{\prime}\leq M\quad\hbox{and}\quad\int_{0}^{T^{\star}}\kappa_{2}(t^{\prime})\,dt^{\prime}\leq M\varepsilon^{3}.

By using Gronwall’s inequality to (
5.9
) with initial conditions (
5.5
), we complete the proof of Lemma
5.2
.
∎

Proposition 5.1
.

Under the assumptions in Theorem
5.1
, for
\varepsilon
being sufficiently small and for any
t\in[0,T^{\star}],
one has

(5.10)

\begin{split}\frac{d}{dt}&\Bigl(\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{2}\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\|u^{S}\|_{V}^{2}\Bigr)+\frac{\nu}{2}\|Au^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
&+\frac{D^{*}}{4}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\Delta c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+z^{2}_{i}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\Delta\Phi^{S}|^{2}\,dx\bigr)+\frac{D^{*}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\nabla\rho^{S}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\\
&\quad\leq\kappa_{3}(t)\bigl(\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\varepsilon^{2}\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|u^{S}\|_{V}^{2}\bigr)+\kappa_{4}(t),\end{split}

where
\kappa_{3}
and
\kappa_{4}
are positive functions satisfying: for some
M
depending only on initial data,
\nu,M_{1},T,\lambda,\Lambda,W(x),\gamma_{i}(x),z_{i}
and
D_{i}
for
i=1,2
, there hold

\int_{0}^{T^{\star}}\kappa_{3}(t^{\prime})\,dt^{\prime}\leq M\quad\hbox{and}\quad\int_{0}^{T^{\star}}\kappa_{4}(t^{\prime})\,dt^{\prime}\leq M\varepsilon^{3}.

Proof.

The main idea of the proof is to take
L^{2}
inner product of
the equation (
5.4a
) with
-\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(\frac{\nabla c_{i}^{S}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\nabla\Phi^{S}).
Indeed we
first get, by using integration by parts, that

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}c_{i}^{S}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(\frac{\nabla c_{i}^{S}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\nabla\Phi^{S})\,dx

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx-\sum_{i=1}^{2}\frac{1}{2}\int_{\Omega}|\nabla c_{i}^{S}|^{2}\partial_{t}\Bigl(\frac{1}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\Bigr)\,dx\ -\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}\rho^{S}\Delta\Phi^{S}\,dx

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dt

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{D^{*}}{16}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\Delta c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+z^{2}_{i}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\Delta\Phi^{S}|^{2}\,dx\bigr)-M\|\partial_{t}N\|^{2}_{L^{2}},

and

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}u^{S}\cdot\nabla c_{i}^{S}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(\frac{\nabla c_{i}^{S}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\nabla\Phi^{S})\,dx

\displaystyle\geq-M\sum_{i=1}^{2}(1+\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{4})\|\nabla c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|u^{S}\|_{V}^{4}-\frac{D^{*}}{16}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\Delta c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+z^{2}_{i}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\Delta\Phi^{S}|^{2}\,dx\bigr).

While for the dissipation term, we find

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits\bigl(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(\frac{\nabla c_{i}^{S}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\nabla\Phi^{S})\bigr)\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(\frac{\nabla c_{i}^{S}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\nabla\Phi^{S})\,dx

\displaystyle=-\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}D_{i}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\bigl(\frac{\Delta c_{i}^{S}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\frac{\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\nabla\Phi^{S}+z_{i}\Delta\Phi^{S}\bigr)\bigl(\frac{\Delta c_{i}^{S}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+\nabla c_{i}^{S}\nabla(\frac{1}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}})+z_{i}\Delta\Phi^{S}\bigr)\,dx

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{D^{*}}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\frac{\Delta c_{i}^{S}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\Delta\Phi^{S}|^{2}\,dx+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}(\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+1)\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}(\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+1)\|\nabla\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=-\frac{D^{*}}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\Delta c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+z^{2}_{i}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\Delta\Phi^{S}|^{2}\,dx\bigr)+\frac{D^{*}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\nabla\rho^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}(\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+1)\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}(\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+1)\|\nabla\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+M\varepsilon^{-2}\|\nabla N\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

For the quadratic term, we have

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}D_{i}\int_{\Omega}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{S}\nabla\Phi^{S})\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(\frac{\nabla c_{i}^{S}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\nabla\Phi^{S})\,dx

\displaystyle\leq M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|\nabla c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}(\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}+1)(\|\Delta c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}})

\displaystyle\leq M\sum_{i=1}^{2}(\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{4}+1)\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{4}\|\nabla c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{D^{*}}{32}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\Delta c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+z^{2}_{i}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\Delta\Phi^{S}|^{2}\,dx\bigr).

The estimates of rest terms are straightforward

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}D_{i}\int_{\Omega}(K_{i}+L_{i}+M_{i})\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(\frac{\nabla c_{i}^{S}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\nabla\Phi^{S})\,dx

\displaystyle\quad\leq M\sum_{i=1}^{2}(\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+1)(\|K_{i}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|L_{i}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|M_{i}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{D^{*}}{32}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\Delta c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+z^{2}_{i}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\Delta\Phi^{S}|^{2}\,dx\bigr).

By summarizing the above estimates, we achieve

(5.11)

\begin{split}\frac{1}{2}&\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\frac{5D^{*}}{16}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\Delta c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+z^{2}_{i}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\Delta\Phi^{S}|^{2}\,dx\bigr)+\frac{D^{*}}{\varepsilon^{2}}\|\nabla\rho^{S}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\\
&\leq M\bigl(\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}(\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{4}+1)(\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{4}+1)\bigr)\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx\\
&\quad+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}(\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{4}+1)\bigl(\|\nabla c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|u^{S}\|_{V}^{4}+\|K_{i}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|L_{i}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|M_{i}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr)\\
&\quad+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}(\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+1)\|\nabla\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+M\|\partial_{t}N\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+M\varepsilon^{-2}\|\nabla N\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.\end{split}

Similarly, we get, taking
L^{2}
inner product of the equation (
5.4c
) with
Au^{S},
that

(5.12)

\begin{split}\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|u^{S}\|_{V}^{2}+\frac{\nu}{2}\|Au^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}&\leq M\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{4}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+M\|u^{S}\|_{V}^{6}+\|O\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
&\quad+\frac{D^{*}}{16}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\Delta c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+z^{2}_{i}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\Delta\Phi^{S}|^{2}\,dx\bigr)+\|P\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.\end{split}

Notice that
\|\nabla c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}+\|u^{S}\|_{V}\leq 3
on
[0,T^{\star}]
, by virtue of (
1.13a
-
1.13c
), Lemma
5.1
, Lemma
5.2
and all the estimates of the layer solutions obtained in Section
4
,
we deduce (
5.10
) from (
5.11
) and (
5.12
).
∎

With Proposition
5.1
, we are able to complete the proof of Theorem
5.1
.

Proof of Theorem
5.1
.

By using Gronwall’s inequality to (
5.10
) with initial conditions (
5.5
), one has (
5.6a
) and (
5.6b
) for
t\leq T^{\star}
. In particular, we have
\|\nabla c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}+\|u^{S}\|_{V}\leq M\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}}
for some
M
irrelevant with
T^{\star}
. Thus by taking
\varepsilon<1
being sufficiently small so that
M\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq\frac{1}{2}
, we conclude that
T^{\star}=T
by the standard continuous argument, which completes the proof of Theorem
5.1
.
∎

5.2.
Estimate of
\|(\partial_{t}c_{i}^{S},\varepsilon\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{S},\partial_{t}u^{S})\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}(L^{2})}
.

The goal of this subsection is to prove the following type of inequality:

(5.13)

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\int_{\Omega}\Bigl(\frac{|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+\varepsilon^{2}|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{S}|^{2}+|\partial_{t}u^{S}|^{2}\Bigr)\,dx+\int_{\Omega}\Bigl(\frac{|\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{S}|^{2}\Bigr)\,dx

\displaystyle+\varepsilon^{2}\|\partial_{t}\Delta\Phi^{S}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\nu\|\partial_{t}u^{S}\|_{V}^{2}\leq O(\varepsilon).

For simplicity, we only n consider the difference functions as follows:

(5.14)

\quad\left\{\begin{array}[]{l}c_{i}^{R}(x^{\prime},y,t)=c^{\varepsilon}_{i}(x^{\prime},y,t)-c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(x^{\prime},y,t)-\varepsilon c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}(x^{\prime},y,t)-\varepsilon^{2}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},y,\tau)\\
\qquad\qquad\qquad-\varepsilon^{2}f(y)c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},\xi,\tau)-\varepsilon^{2}g(y)c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},\eta,\tau),\\
\Phi^{R}(x^{\prime},y,t)=\Phi^{\varepsilon}(x^{\prime},y,t)-\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(x^{\prime},y,t)-\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(x^{\prime},y,\tau)-\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}(x^{\prime},y,\tau),\\
u^{R}(x^{\prime},y,t)=u^{\varepsilon}(x^{\prime},y,t)-u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(x^{\prime},y,t),\end{array}\right.

which satisfy the following boundary conditions:

(5.15)

c_{i}^{R}|_{\partial\Omega}=0,\quad\Phi^{R}|_{\partial\Omega}=0\quad\hbox{and}\quad u^{R}|_{\partial\Omega}=0.

Remark 5.1
.

Based on (
5.13
), the construction of
(c_{i}^{R},\Phi^{R},u^{R})
guarantees homogeneous Dirichlet boundary conditions and collects all the layer solutions appearing in the left-hand side of (
5.13
). Though the function
\varepsilon^{2}f(y)c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},\xi,\tau)
,
\varepsilon^{2}gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},\eta,\tau)
are too “small” to appear in the energy estimate (
5.13
), they compensate the boundary value of
\varepsilon^{2}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(x^{\prime},y,\tau)
.

Under the assumption of Theorem
5.1
, we have

(5.16a)

\displaystyle\|\nabla c_{i}^{R}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}(L^{2})}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|u^{R}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}(V)}+\varepsilon\|\Delta\Phi^{R}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}(L^{2})}\leq M_{2}\varepsilon^{\frac{3}{2}},

(5.16b)

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta c_{i}^{R}\|_{L^{2}_{T}(L^{2})}+\|Au^{R}\|_{L^{2}_{T}(L^{2})}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\Phi^{R}\|_{L^{2}_{T}(L^{2})}\leq M_{2}\varepsilon.

It is easy to observe that
(c_{i}^{R},\Phi^{R},u^{R})
verifies

(5.17a)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}+u^{R}\cdot\nabla c_{i}^{R}-\varepsilon z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)})=D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(\frac{\nabla c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\nabla\Phi^{R}))

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{R}\nabla\Phi^{R})+E_{i}+F_{i}+G_{i}+H_{i},

(5.17b)

\displaystyle\qquad\rho^{R}=-\varepsilon^{2}\Delta\Phi^{R}-\varepsilon^{2}\Delta\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}-\varepsilon^{3}\Delta\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}f\partial_{\xi}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}g\partial_{\eta}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},

(5.17c)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}u^{R}+(u^{R}+u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)})\cdot\nabla u^{R}-\nu\Delta u^{R}-\nabla p^{R}=-u^{R}\cdot\nabla u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-(\rho^{R}+\varepsilon^{2}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}f\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}g\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\nabla(\Phi^{R}+\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}),

(5.17d)

\displaystyle\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits u^{R}=0,

where

\displaystyle E_{i}\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}-u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\cdot\nabla c_{i}^{R}-u^{R}\cdot\nabla(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})

\displaystyle\qquad+z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits\big((\varepsilon c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\nabla\Phi^{R}\big)

\displaystyle\qquad+z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits\big(c_{i}^{R}\nabla(\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})\big),

\displaystyle F_{i}\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits\bigl((c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}-c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0))\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\bigr)-z_{i}D_{i}\gamma_{i}(x^{\prime},0,0)f\partial_{\xi}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}-z_{i}D_{i}\gamma_{i}(x^{\prime},1,0)g\partial_{\eta}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)},

\displaystyle G_{i}\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}\varepsilon u^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\cdot\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}-\varepsilon z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\nabla\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})+2\varepsilon D_{i}(f^{\prime}\partial_{\xi}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}-g^{\prime}\partial_{\eta}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}),

\displaystyle H_{i}\mathrel{\mathop{\kern 0.0pt=}\limits^{\hbox{\footnotesize def}}}-\varepsilon^{2}u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\cdot\nabla(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})+\varepsilon^{2}D_{i}\Delta c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}

\displaystyle\qquad+\varepsilon^{2}D_{i}(f\Delta_{x^{\prime}}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\Delta_{x^{\prime}}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})+\varepsilon^{2}D_{i}(f^{\prime\prime}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g^{\prime\prime}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})

\displaystyle\qquad+\varepsilon^{2}z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})+\varepsilon^{2}z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits\bigl((c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\nabla(\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})\bigr).

By taking time derivative of equation (
5.17a
) and (
5.17c
), we get

(5.18)

\begin{split}\partial_{t}^{2}c_{i}^{R}+&\partial_{t}u^{R}\cdot\nabla c_{i}^{R}+u^{R}\cdot\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}-\varepsilon^{-1}z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)})\\
&=D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(\frac{\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}))+z_{i}D_{i}\partial_{t}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{R}\nabla\Phi^{R})\\
