&\quad+z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\nabla\Phi^{R})+\varepsilon z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)})\\
&\quad+\partial_{t}E_{i}+\partial_{t}F_{i}+\partial_{t}G_{i}+\partial_{t}H_{i},\end{split}

and

(5.19)

\begin{split}&\partial_{t}^{2}u^{R}+(u^{R}+u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)})\cdot\nabla\partial_{t}u^{R}-\nu\Delta\partial_{t}u^{R}-\nabla\partial_{t}p^{R}\\
&\quad=-\partial_{t}(u^{R}+u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)})\cdot\nabla u^{R}-\partial_{t}u^{R}\cdot\nabla u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}-u^{R}\cdot\nabla\partial_{t}u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\\
&\qquad-(\partial_{t}\rho^{R}+\partial_{\tau}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\partial_{\tau}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\partial_{\tau}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\nabla(\Phi^{R}+\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})\\
&\qquad-(\rho^{R}+\varepsilon^{2}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}f\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}g\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\nabla(\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}+\partial_{t}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon^{-2}\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon^{-1}\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}).\end{split}

Theorem 5.2
.

Under the assumptions in Theorem
5.1
, if we assume in addition, that

(5.20a)

\displaystyle\|c_{i}^{R}(0)\|_{L^{2}}\leq M_{1}\varepsilon^{2},

(5.20b)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}(0)\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{t}u^{R}(0)\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon\|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}(0)\|_{L^{2}}\leq M_{1}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Then for
\varepsilon
being sufficiently small, there exists a positive constant
M_{2}
depending only on initial data,
\nu,M_{1},T,\lambda,\Lambda,W(x),\gamma_{i}(x),z_{i}
and
D_{i}
for
i=1,2
, so that

(5.21a)

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}(L^{2})}+\|\partial_{t}u^{R}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}(L^{2})}+\varepsilon\|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}(L^{2})}\leq M_{2}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}},

(5.21b)

\displaystyle\|\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}\|_{L^{2}_{T}(L^{2})}+\|\nabla\partial_{t}u^{R}\|_{L^{2}_{T}(L^{2})}+\|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\|_{L^{2}_{T}(L^{2})}+\varepsilon\|\Delta\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\|_{L^{2}_{T}(L^{2})}\leq M_{2}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Since Theorem
5.2
is a direct consequence of the following estimate (
5.22
) and Gronwall’s inequality, it suffices to prove (
5.22
).

Proposition 5.2
.

Under the assumptions in Theorem
5.2
, for any
t\in[0,T]
: one has

(5.22)

\begin{split}\frac{d}{dt}&\Bigl(\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\|\partial_{t}u^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Bigr)+\frac{\nu}{4}\|\nabla\partial_{t}u^{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\\
&+\frac{D^{*}}{8}\Bigl(\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}^{2}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}|^{2}\,dx\Bigr)+\frac{D^{*}\varepsilon^{2}}{2}\|\Delta\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\\
\leq&\kappa_{5}(t)\sup_{[0,t]}(\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{t}u^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})+\kappa_{6}(t),\end{split}

where
\kappa_{5},\kappa_{6}
are positive functions satisfying

\int_{0}^{T}\kappa_{5}(t^{\prime})\,dt^{\prime}\sim O(1)\quad\hbox{and}\quad\int_{0}^{T}\kappa_{6}(t^{\prime})\,dt^{\prime}\sim O(\varepsilon),\quad\textit{as}\ \varepsilon\rightarrow 0.

Proof.

By taking
L^{2}
inner product of the equation (
5.19
) with

\partial_{t}u^{R}
and using integration by parts, we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|\partial_{t}u^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\nu\|\nabla\partial_{t}u^{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq\|Au^{R}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{t}u^{R}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\partial_{t}u^{R}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{t}u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|Au^{R}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{t}u^{R}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\nabla u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{t}u^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\partial_{t}u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|u^{R}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{t}u^{R}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\Delta\Phi^{R}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{t}\rho^{R}\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla\partial_{t}u^{R}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\Phi^{R}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{\tau}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\partial_{\tau}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\partial_{\tau}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{t}u^{R}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\nabla(\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{t}\rho^{R}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{t}u^{R}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\nabla(\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{\tau}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\partial_{\tau}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\partial_{\tau}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{t}u^{R}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\rho^{R}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{t}u^{R}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\varepsilon^{2}\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\varepsilon^{-2}\rho^{R}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{t}u^{R}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{2}\|\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\|_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{t}u^{R}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}\|\varepsilon^{2}\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{t}u^{R}\|_{L^{2}}.

Applying Hölder’s inequality yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\|\partial_{t}u^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\nu\|\nabla\partial_{t}u^{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq M\Bigl(1+\frac{1}{\nu}+\frac{1}{\nu}\|Au^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{H^{3}}^{2}+\|\nabla u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\rho^{R}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\|\varepsilon^{-2}\rho^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\varepsilon^{4}\|\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\|\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Bigr)\|\partial_{t}u^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+M(\frac{1}{\nu}\|\Delta\Phi^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla(\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2})\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{3\nu}{4}\|\nabla\partial_{t}u^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{D^{*}}{16}\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}^{2}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad+M\bigl(\|Au^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\Phi^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\partial_{\tau}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\partial_{\tau}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\partial_{\tau}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\bigr)

\displaystyle\quad+M\|\nabla(\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\partial_{\tau}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\partial_{\tau}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\partial_{\tau}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+M\|\varepsilon^{2}\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}.

By inserting (
1.13a
-
1.13c
), (
5.16a
-
5.16b
) and all the estimates of the layer solutions obtained in Section
4
into the above inequality, we achieve

(5.23)

\begin{split}\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\partial_{t}u^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\nu}{4}\|\nabla\partial_{t}u^{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\leq&\kappa_{7}(t)\bigl(\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\|\partial_{t}u^{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\bigr)\\
&+\frac{D^{*}}{16}\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}^{2}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}|^{2}\,dx+\kappa_{8}(t),\end{split}

where
\kappa_{7}
,
\kappa_{8}
are positive functions satisfying

\int_{0}^{T}\kappa_{7}(t^{\prime})\,dt^{\prime}\sim O(1)\quad\hbox{and}\quad\int_{0}^{T}\kappa_{8}(t^{\prime})\,dt^{\prime}\sim O(\varepsilon^{2}),\quad\textit{as $\varepsilon\rightarrow 0$}.

Below let us handle the estimate of
(\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R},\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}).
We shall first take
L^{2}
inner product
of the equation (
5.18
) with
\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}

and then summarizing the resulting equalities for
i=1,2.

Indeed by virtue of
\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}|_{\partial\Omega}=0
and Poincaré’s inequality, one has

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}

\displaystyle\partial_{t}^{2}c_{i}^{R}(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx=\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\partial_{t}^{2}c_{i}^{R}\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}^{2}\rho^{R}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\,dx

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}\partial_{t}(\frac{1}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}})\,dx

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon^{2}\int_{\Omega}\nabla\partial_{t}^{2}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\,dx-\varepsilon^{-1}\int_{\Omega}\Delta\partial_{\tau}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad-\varepsilon^{-1}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{\xi}\partial_{\tau}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(f\partial_{y}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}+f^{\prime}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx^{\prime}dy

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{-1}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{\eta}\partial_{\tau}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(g\partial_{y}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}+g^{\prime}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx^{\prime}dy

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\frac{\varepsilon^{2}}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad-M\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx-\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}-\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla\partial_{t}^{2}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad-M\varepsilon^{-2}\|\partial_{\xi}\partial_{\tau}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}-M\varepsilon^{-2}\|\partial_{\eta}\partial_{\tau}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{D^{*}}{16}\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}^{2}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}|^{2}\,dx-\varepsilon^{-1}\int_{\Omega}\Delta\partial_{\tau}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\,dx.

Recalling that
\partial_{\tau}^{2}\Delta\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}=-\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}^{2}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}(0)\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}).

We get, by using integration by parts, that

\displaystyle-

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}D_{i}\int_{\Omega}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)})(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx

\displaystyle=\varepsilon^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}D_{i}\int_{\Omega}(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)})\nabla(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}})\,dx

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{-1}\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}^{2}D_{i}\int_{\Omega}\Big((c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}-c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0))\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}-c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}(0))\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\Big)\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon^{-1}\int_{\Omega}\Delta\partial_{\tau}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\,dx

\displaystyle\geq-M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{-2}\|c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{-2}\|c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr)\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad-M\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\bigr)\|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad-M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{-2}\|c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{-2}\|c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr)

\displaystyle\quad-\|\tau\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}-\|\tau\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{-1}\int_{\Omega}\Delta\partial_{\tau}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\,dx.

While for the dissipation term, one has

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}

\displaystyle D_{i}\int_{\Omega}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(\frac{\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}))(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx

\displaystyle=-\sum_{i=1}^{2}D_{i}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\frac{\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}|^{2}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{i=1}^{2}D_{i}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(\frac{\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}\nabla(\frac{1}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}})\,dx

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{D^{*}}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\frac{\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}|^{2}\,dx+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{D^{*}}{2}\Bigl(\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}^{2}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}|^{2}\,dx+2\varepsilon^{2}\|\Delta\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\Bigr)

\displaystyle\quad-\varepsilon^{2}D^{*}\int_{\Omega}\Delta\partial_{t}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\Delta\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\,dx-\varepsilon D^{*}\int_{\Omega}\Delta\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\Delta\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad-\int_{\Omega}(f\partial_{\tau}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\partial_{\tau}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\Delta\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\,dx+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\bigr)

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{D^{*}}{2}\Bigl(\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}^{2}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}|^{2}\,dx\Bigr)-\frac{D^{*}\varepsilon^{2}}{2}\|\Delta\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+M\varepsilon^{2}\|\Delta\partial_{t}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+M\|\Delta\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+M\varepsilon^{-2}\bigl(\|\partial_{\tau}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\partial_{\tau}\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr).

For the quadratic terms, noting that
\|c_{i}^{R}\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim 1
, we get, by using integration by parts and Hölder’s inequality, that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}u^{R}\cdot\nabla c_{i}^{R}(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx

\displaystyle=-\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}u^{R}\cdot\frac{\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}c_{i}^{R}\,dx-\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}u^{R}\cdot\nabla(\frac{1}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}})\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}c_{i}^{R}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad-\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}u^{R}\cdot\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\rho^{R}\,dx

\displaystyle\geq-M\|\partial_{t}u^{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}-M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{D^{*}}{32}\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}^{2}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}|^{2}\,dx\Big).

Thanks to
\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits u^{R}=0
, one has

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}u^{R}\cdot\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}u^{R}\cdot\nabla(\frac{1}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}})|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}\,dx-\int_{\Omega}u^{R}\cdot\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\partial_{t}\rho^{R}\,dx

\displaystyle\geq-M\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|u^{R}\|_{V}\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{3}{2}}-M\|u^{R}\|_{V}\|\partial_{t}\rho^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\partial_{t}\rho^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq-M(\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{4}+1)\|u^{R}\|^{4}_{V}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{D^{*}}{32}\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}^{2}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}|^{2}\,dx\Big).

Whereas for the term
\partial_{t}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{R}\nabla\Phi^{R})
, one has

\begin{split}\sum_{i=1}^{2}&z_{i}D_{i}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{R}\nabla\Phi^{R})(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx\\
&=-\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}D_{i}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}\nabla\Phi^{R}\nabla(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx\\
&\quad-\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}D_{i}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{R}\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\nabla(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx,\\
\end{split}

from which, we infer

\begin{split}\sum_{i=1}^{2}&z_{i}D_{i}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{R}\nabla\Phi^{R})(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx\\
&\leq M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\|\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta\Phi^{R}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla c_{i}^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta c_{i}^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\|_{L^{2}}\bigr)\\
&\qquad\times\bigl(1+\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\bigr)\bigl(\|\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\|_{L^{2}}\bigr)\\
&\leq M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(1+\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{4}\bigr)\|\Delta\Phi^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{4}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx\\
&\quad+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(1+\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{4}\bigr)\|\nabla c_{i}^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\Delta c_{i}^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\\
&\quad+\frac{D^{*}}{32}\Big(\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}^{2}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}|^{2}\,dx\Big).\end{split}

The estimates of the rest terms are much more straightforward. Precisely, by using integration by parts and Hölder’s inequality, one has

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\bigl(z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\nabla\Phi^{R})+\varepsilon z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)})\bigr)\bigl(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\bigr)\,dx

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\bigr)\bigl(\|\nabla\Phi^{R}\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon\|\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon\|\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{L^{2}}\bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\bigl(1+\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\bigr)\bigl(\|\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\|_{L^{2}}\bigr)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{D^{*}}{32}\bigl(\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}^{2}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}|^{2}\,dx\bigr)

\displaystyle+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\bigr)\bigl(1+\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\bigr)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\bigl(\|\nabla\Phi^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon\|\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr).

Similarly, one has

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}E_{i}(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}(\partial_{t}u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}c_{i}^{R}+u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R})\cdot\nabla(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\partial_{t}u^{R}\cdot\nabla(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}(\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\partial_{\tau}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\partial_{\tau}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\partial_{\tau}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})u^{R}\cdot\nabla(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}D_{i}\int_{\Omega}(\varepsilon c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\nabla(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}D_{i}\int_{\Omega}(\varepsilon\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\partial_{\tau}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\partial_{\tau}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\partial_{\tau}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\nabla\Phi^{R}\nabla(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}D_{i}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}\nabla(\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})\nabla(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}D_{i}\int_{\Omega}(\int_{0}^{1}\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}(\theta t)d\theta)\tau\nabla(\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{t}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})\nabla(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}D_{i}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{R}(0)\nabla(\partial_{t}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon^{-2}\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon^{-1}\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})\nabla(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx,

from which, we infer

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}E_{i}(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx

\displaystyle\leq M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(1+\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\bigr)\bigl(\|u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\|\nabla(\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\|\tau\nabla(\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{t}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\bigr)\sup_{[0,t]}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx

\displaystyle\quad+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(1+\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\bigr)\|c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\partial_{t}u^{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+M\varepsilon^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(1+\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\bigr)\|c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{D^{*}}{32}\Bigl(\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}^{2}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}|^{2}\,dx\Bigr)

\displaystyle\quad+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(1+\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\bigr)\bigl(\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\partial_{\tau}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\partial_{\tau}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\partial_{\tau}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|u^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\|\partial_{t}u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|c_{i}^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\varepsilon\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\partial_{\tau}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\partial_{\tau}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\partial_{\tau}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\nabla\Phi^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\|c_{i}^{R}(0)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla(\partial_{t}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon^{-2}\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon^{-1}\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\bigr).

Along the same line, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}F_{i}(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx

\displaystyle=-\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}D_{i}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\nabla(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}D_{i}\int_{\Omega}(\int_{0}^{t}\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(\theta s)ds)\tau\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\nabla(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}D_{i}\gamma_{i}(x^{\prime},0,0)\varepsilon^{-1}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{\xi}\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\partial_{y}\bigl(f(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\bigr)\,dx

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}D_{i}\gamma_{i}(x^{\prime},1,0)\varepsilon^{-1}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{\eta}\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\partial_{y}\bigl(g(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\bigr)\,dx

\displaystyle\leq\frac{D^{*}}{32}\Bigl(\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}^{2}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}|^{2}\,dx\Bigr)

\displaystyle\quad+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(1+\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\bigr)\bigl(\|\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}+\|\tau\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\quad+\varepsilon^{-2}\|\partial_{\xi}\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon^{-2}\|\partial_{\eta}\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\bigr),

and

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}G_{i}(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx

\displaystyle\leq\frac{D^{*}}{32}\Bigl(\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}^{2}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}|^{2}\,dx\Bigr)

\displaystyle\quad+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(1+\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\bigr)\bigl(\varepsilon^{2}\|\partial_{t}(u^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{2}\|\partial_{t}(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\nabla\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\varepsilon^{-2}\|\partial_{\xi}\partial_{\tau}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{-2}\|\partial_{\eta}\partial_{\tau}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigr),

and

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\partial_{t}H_{i}(\frac{\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}+z_{i}\partial_{t}\Phi^{R})\,dx

\displaystyle\leq\frac{D^{*}}{32}\Bigl(\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}^{2}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}|^{2}\,dx\Bigr)

\displaystyle\quad+M\sum_{i=1}^{2}\bigl(1+\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\bigr)\bigl(\varepsilon^{2}\|\partial_{t}u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\|u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\partial_{\tau}(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla\partial_{\tau}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|\nabla_{x^{\prime}}\partial_{\tau}(fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\|\partial_{\tau}(f^{\prime\prime}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g^{\prime\prime}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\varepsilon^{2}\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\nabla\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\|\nabla\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\|\partial_{\tau}(c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla(\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\|c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\|\nabla(\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{t}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)})\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}\bigr).

By summing up the above inequalities and using (
1.13a
-
1.13c
), (
5.16a
-
5.16b
) and all the estimates concerning the layer solutions, which have been obtained in Section
4
, we achieve

(5.24)

\begin{split}&\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\varepsilon^{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\\
&\quad+\frac{3D^{*}}{16}\Bigl(\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}^{2}\int_{\Omega}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}|^{2}\,dx\Bigr)+\frac{D^{*}\varepsilon^{2}}{2}\|\Delta\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\\
&\leq\kappa_{9}(t)\sup_{[0,t]}(\sum_{i=1}^{2}\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{R}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx+\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{R}\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{t}u^{R}\|_{L^{2}}^{2})+\kappa_{10}(t),\end{split}

where
\kappa_{9},\kappa_{10}
are positive functions satisfying

\int_{0}^{T}\kappa_{9}(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}\sim O(1)\quad\hbox{and}\quad\int_{0}^{T}\kappa_{10}(t^{\prime})dt^{\prime}\sim O(\varepsilon),\quad\textit{as}\ \varepsilon\rightarrow 0.

By combining (
5.23
) and (
5.24
) together, we thus complete the proof of Proposition
5.2
.
∎

Remark 5.2
.

Recalling the definition of
(c_{i}^{R},\Phi^{R},u^{R})
and
(c_{i}^{S},\Phi^{S},u^{S})
, we deduce that

(5.25)

\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon\|\partial_{t}\nabla\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{t}u^{S}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}.

5.3.
Estimate of
\|\Delta c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}(L^{2})}
.

Firstly we rewrite (
5.4a
) as

\displaystyle D_{i}\Delta c_{i}^{S}=\partial_{t}c_{i}^{S}+u^{S}\cdot\nabla c_{i}^{S}-z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\nabla\Phi^{S})-z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{S}\nabla\Phi^{S})-K_{i}-L_{i}-M_{i},

from which, we infer

\displaystyle\|\Delta c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}(1+\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}})+\|K_{i}\|_{L^{2}}+\|L_{i}\|_{L^{2}}+\|M_{i}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\|\nabla c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\Delta c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\|u^{S}\|_{V}+\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}).

Applying Hölder inequality gives rise to

\displaystyle\|\Delta c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}(1+\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}})+\|K_{i}\|_{L^{2}}+\|L_{i}\|_{L^{2}}+\|M_{i}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad\|\nabla c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{2}}(\|u^{S}\|_{V}^{2}+\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}),

which together with (
1.13a
-
1.13c
), (
5.6a
-
5.6b
), (
5.21a
-
5.21b
), Lemma
5.1
and all the estimates of the layer solutions obtained in Section
4
implies the following Corollary:

Corollary 5.1
.

Under the assumptions in Theorem
5.1
and Theorem
5.2
, one has

\|c_{i}^{S}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}(H^{2})}+\|\rho^{S}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}(H^{2})}\lesssim\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}},

and consequently

\|c^{\varepsilon}_{i}-c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}(H^{2})}+\|\rho^{\varepsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}_{T}(H^{2})}\lesssim\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Now we are in a position to complete the proof of Theorem
.

Proof of Theorem
1.2
.

It suffices to verify the assumptions in Corollary
5.1
. First of all, thanks to (
1.14b
), Theorem
2.1
implies the existence of
T
, which equals
+\infty
in the case
d=2
, so that the systems (
1.4
) and (
1.6
) have unique
strong
solution on
[0,T].

Then in view of (
1.14d
), (
4.10
) and (
4.11
), one has

\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(0)=\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(0)=0.

Furthermore, due to (
1.14a
) and
\Phi^{\varepsilon}(0)=\Phi_{W}=\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)
, we have

\displaystyle c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}(0)=c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(0)=c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(0)=c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(0)=c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(0)=0,

\displaystyle\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}(0)=\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(0)=\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}(0)=\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}(0)=0\quad\hbox{and}\quad u^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}(0)=u^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}(0)=0,

which together with the analysis in Section
4
ensures that

\displaystyle c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}=c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}=c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}=c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}=c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}=0,

\displaystyle\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}=\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}=\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}=\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}=0,\quad u^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}=u^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}=0,

and

(5.26)

\begin{gathered}c_{i}^{S}(0)=c_{i}^{R}(0)=c^{\varepsilon}_{i}(0)-c_{i}(0),\quad\Phi^{S}(0)=\Phi^{R}(0)=\psi^{\varepsilon}(0)-\psi(0)=0,\\
u^{S}(0)=u^{R}(0)=u^{\varepsilon}(0)-u_{0},\quad L_{i}(0)=M_{i}(0)=N(0)=P(0)=0,\\
O(0)=-u^{S}(0)\cdot\nabla u_{0}-u_{0}\cdot\nabla u^{S}(0),\\
K_{i}(0)=-u_{0}\cdot\nabla c_{i}^{S}(0)-u^{S}(0)\cdot\nabla c_{i}(0)+z_{i}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits\bigl(c_{i}^{S}(0)\nabla\Phi_{W}\bigr),\\
\partial_{t}N=-2\varepsilon^{3}\partial_{t}(f^{\prime}\partial_{\xi}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}-g^{\prime}\partial_{\eta}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})-\varepsilon^{4}\partial_{t}(f\Delta_{x^{\prime}}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\Delta_{x^{\prime}}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f^{\prime\prime}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g^{\prime\prime}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}).\end{gathered}

Thus all the assumptions in Theorem
5.2
and Theorem
5.1
hold except (
5.20b
).

Next we verify (
5.20b
). In view of (
4.10
) and (
4.11
), we infer

(5.27)

\begin{gathered}\|O(0)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|u_{0}\|_{H^{3}}\|u^{S}(0)\|_{V},\quad\|\partial_{t}N(0)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\varepsilon^{\frac{7}{2}}\|\partial_{t}\psi(0)\|_{H^{2}},\\
\|K_{i}(0)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|u_{0}\|_{H^{2}}\|\nabla c_{i}^{S}(0)\|_{L^{2}}+\|c_{i}(0)\|_{H^{3}}\|u^{S}(0)\|_{L^{2}}+\|\Phi_{W}\|_{H^{4}}\|\nabla c_{i}^{S}(0)\|_{L^{2}}.\end{gathered}

By virtue of the equation (
5.4a
) and (
5.26
), one has

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{S}(0)\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|u^{S}(0)\|_{V}\|\Delta c_{i}^{S}(0)\|_{L^{2}}+\|\Delta c_{i}^{S}(0)\|_{L^{2}}+\|K_{i}(0)\|_{L^{2}}.

Notice that
\rho^{S}(0)=\rho^{\varepsilon}(0)=0
, we deduce from equation (
5.4c
) that

\displaystyle\|\partial_{t}u^{S}(0)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|Au^{S}(0)\|_{L^{2}}+\|Au^{S}(0)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\|O(0)\|_{L^{2}}.

To estimate
\|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}
, we get, by first multiplying the equation of
c_{i}^{S}
by
z_{i}
and summing up for
i=1,2
, and then inserting the equation (
5.4b
) into the resulting equation, that

\displaystyle-\varepsilon^{2}\Delta\partial_{t}\Phi^{S}+\partial_{t}N+u^{S}\cdot\nabla\rho^{S}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}D_{i}\Delta c_{i}^{S}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}^{2}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\nabla\Phi^{S})

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}^{2}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{S}\nabla\Phi^{S})+\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}(K_{i}+L_{i}+M_{i}),

from which, one has

\displaystyle\Delta(\varepsilon^{2}\partial_{t}\Phi^{S}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}D_{i}c_{i}^{S}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}^{2}D_{i}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\Phi^{S})=u^{S}\cdot\nabla\rho^{S}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}^{2}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(\Phi^{S}\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}^{2}D_{i}\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits(c_{i}^{S}\nabla\Phi^{S})-\sum_{i=1}^{2}z_{i}(K_{i}+L_{i}+M_{i})+\partial_{t}N.

It follows from standard elliptic estimate and
\Phi^{S}(0)=0=\rho^{S}(0)
that

\displaystyle\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{S}(0)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|\nabla c_{i}^{S}(0)\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{t}N(0)\|_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=1}^{2}\|K_{i}(0)\|_{L^{2}}.

Finally, by inserting (
1.14a
-
1.14c
), Lemma
5.1
and (
5.27
) into the above inequalities, we achieve

\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{S}(0)\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{t}u^{S}(0)\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon\|\nabla\partial_{t}\Phi^{S}(0)\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}.

This verifies (
5.20b
) and we thus completes the proof of Theorem
1.2
.
∎

Appendix A
The proof of Lemma
5.1

The goal of this section is to present the proof of
Lemma
5.1
.

Proof of
Lemma
5.1
.

The estimates of the terms
K_{i}
to
P_{i}
are straight application of Hölder’s inequality.
We first observe that

\begin{split}\|&K_{i}\|_{L^{2}}\leq M\|u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon u^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}(\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&+M\|c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\|_{H^{2}}\|u^{S}\|_{H^{1}}\\
&+M\|\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}f\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}g\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{W^{2,\infty}}(\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx)^{\frac{1}{2}}\\
&+M\varepsilon\|c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{W^{1,\infty}}\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}.\end{split}

For
L_{i}
, one has

\|L_{i}\|_{L^{2}}\leq M\varepsilon\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\bigl(\|\xi\partial^{2}_{\xi}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\xi\partial^{2}_{\eta}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}\bigr)+M\varepsilon^{2}\|\partial_{t}c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{H^{2}}\|\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{H^{2}}.

For the estimate of
M_{i}
, we find

\displaystyle\|M_{i}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\varepsilon^{3}\|u^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\cdot\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon^{2}\|u^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\cdot\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{2}}+M\varepsilon^{2}\|\Delta c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\varepsilon\|u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon u^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla(\varepsilon c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+M\varepsilon^{2}\|f\Delta_{x^{\prime}}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\Delta_{x^{\prime}}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\Delta_{x^{\prime}}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\Delta_{x^{\prime}}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+M\varepsilon^{2}\|f^{\prime\prime}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g^{\prime\prime}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f^{\prime\prime}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g^{\prime\prime}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+M\varepsilon\|f^{\prime}\partial_{\xi}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}-g^{\prime}\partial_{\eta}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f^{\prime}\partial_{\xi}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}-\varepsilon g^{\prime}\partial_{\eta}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+M\varepsilon^{2}\|\nabla c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla(f\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+M\varepsilon^{2}\|c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|f^{\prime\prime}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g^{\prime\prime}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\Delta_{x^{\prime}}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\Delta_{x^{\prime}}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+M\varepsilon\|c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|f^{\prime}\partial_{\xi}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}-\varepsilon g^{\prime}\partial_{\eta}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}+M\varepsilon\|c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}\|_{H^{2}}\|\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{H^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+M\varepsilon^{3}\bigl(\|c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{H^{2}}\|\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{H^{2}}+\|c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{H^{2}}\|\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{H^{2}}+\varepsilon\|c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{H^{2}}\|\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{H^{2}}\bigr)

\displaystyle\quad+M\varepsilon\|c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{H^{1}}\|\nabla(\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}f\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}g\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+M\varepsilon\|c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\Delta(\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}f\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}g\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\|_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+M\varepsilon\|\nabla(\varepsilon fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\|_{L^{2}}\|\nabla(\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}f\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}g\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\quad+M\varepsilon^{2}\|fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\|_{L^{2}}\|\Delta(\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}f\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}g\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\quad+M\varepsilon\|\nabla(\varepsilon c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\|_{L^{2}}\|\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{H^{1}}

\displaystyle\quad+M\varepsilon^{2}\|c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+fc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+gc_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\|_{L^{2}}\|\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{H^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+M\varepsilon^{2}\bigl(\|\partial_{t}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{t}c_{\scriptscriptstyle i,RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}\bigr)+M\|\partial_{\xi}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}+M\|\partial_{\eta}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}.

For
N_{i}
, we have

\begin{split}\|N_{i}\|_{L^{2}}&\leq\varepsilon^{3}\|\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{H^{2}}+\varepsilon^{4}\|\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{H^{2}}+\varepsilon^{3}\|\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{H^{2}}+M\varepsilon^{3}\bigl(\|\partial_{\xi}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{\eta}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}\bigr)\\
&\quad+\varepsilon^{4}\|f\Delta_{x^{\prime}}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\Delta_{x^{\prime}}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f^{\prime\prime}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g^{\prime\prime}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon^{2}\|f\partial_{\xi}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\partial_{\eta}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}},\end{split}

and

\begin{split}\|\nabla N_{i}\|_{L^{2}}&\leq\varepsilon^{3}\|\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{H^{3}}+\varepsilon^{4}\|\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{H^{3}}+\varepsilon^{3}\|\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{H^{3}}+M\varepsilon^{3}\bigl(\|\nabla_{x^{\prime}}\partial_{\xi}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla_{x^{\prime}}\partial_{\eta}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}\bigr)\\
&\quad+\varepsilon^{4}\|f\nabla_{x^{\prime}}\Delta_{x^{\prime}}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\nabla_{x^{\prime}}\Delta_{x^{\prime}}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f^{\prime\prime}\nabla_{x^{\prime}}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g^{\prime\prime}\nabla_{x^{\prime}}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\quad+M\varepsilon^{2}\bigl(\|\partial_{\xi}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{\eta}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}\bigr)\\
&\quad+\varepsilon^{3}\|\partial_{\xi}(f\Delta_{x^{\prime}}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f^{\prime\prime}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})-\partial_{\eta}(g\Delta_{x^{\prime}}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g^{\prime\prime}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\quad+M\varepsilon^{2}\bigl(\|\nabla_{x^{\prime}}\partial_{\xi}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\nabla_{x^{\prime}}\partial_{\eta}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}\bigr)+\varepsilon\|\partial_{\xi}(f\partial_{\xi}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})-\partial_{\eta}(g\partial_{\eta}^{2}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\|_{L^{2}},\end{split}

and

\begin{split}\|\partial_{t}N_{i}\|_{L^{2}}&\leq\varepsilon^{3}\|\partial_{t}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{H^{2}}+\varepsilon^{4}\|\partial_{t}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{H^{2}}+\varepsilon\|\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{H^{2}}+M\varepsilon^{3}\bigl(\|\partial_{\xi}\partial_{t}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}+\|\partial_{\eta}\partial_{t}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}\bigr)\\
&\quad+\varepsilon^{4}\|f\Delta_{x^{\prime}}\partial_{t}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\Delta_{x^{\prime}}\partial_{t}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f^{\prime\prime}\partial_{t}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g^{\prime\prime}\partial_{t}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\quad+\|f\partial_{\xi}^{2}\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\partial_{\eta}^{2}\partial_{\tau}\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{2}}.\end{split}

For
O_{i}
, one has

\begin{split}&\|O_{i}\|_{L^{2}}\leq\|u^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon u^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{H^{2}}\|u^{S}\|_{H^{1}}\\
&\quad+M\varepsilon^{2}\|\rho^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\Delta\Phi^{S}\|_{L^{2}}\\
&\quad+M\|\nabla(\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}f\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}g\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\|_{L^{\infty}}\Bigl(\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla c_{i}^{S}|^{2}}{c_{i}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}}\,dx\Bigr)^{\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

For
P_{i}
, we have

\begin{split}\|P_{i}\|_{L^{2}}&\leq\varepsilon^{2}\|u^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\nabla u^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}\|_{L^{2}}+\varepsilon^{2}\|\rho^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+f\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle LM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+g\rho_{\scriptscriptstyle RM}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}\|_{L^{\infty}}\\
&\qquad\quad\times\|\nabla(\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}\Phi^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(0)}+\varepsilon\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle I}^{\scriptscriptstyle(1)}+\varepsilon^{2}f\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle LB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)}+\varepsilon^{2}g\Phi_{\scriptscriptstyle RB}^{\scriptscriptstyle(2)})\|_{L^{2}}.\end{split}

By summarizing the above estimates and using the estimates which we obtained in the previous sections, we arrive at
(
5.7
). This completes the proof of Lemma
5.1
.
∎
